Project Title: E-commerce Order Analysis
Project Overview:
Analyze order patterns, revenue growth, and customer preferences for an e-commerce business.

SQL Tasks:
• Retrieve top 5 highest-selling products.

=>
mysql> SELECT
    ->     sku,
    ->     category,
    ->     SUM(revenue) AS total_revenue
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY sku, category
    -> ORDER BY total_revenue DESC
    -> LIMIT 5;
+--------------+---------------+---------------+
| sku          | category      | total_revenue |
+--------------+---------------+---------------+
| J0230-SKD-M  | Set           |        483137 |
| JNE3797-KR-L | Western Dress |        453655 |
| J0230-SKD-S  | Set           |        448723 |
| JNE3797-KR-M | Western Dress |        400569 |
| JNE3797-KR-S | Western Dress |        344409 |
+--------------+---------------+---------------+


• Calculate monthly revenue trends.

=>
mysql> SELECT
    ->     DATE_FORMAT(date, '%Y-%m') AS month,
    ->     SUM(revenue) AS monthly_revenue
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY DATE_FORMAT(date, '%Y-%m')
    -> ORDER BY month;
+---------+-----------------+
| month   | monthly_revenue |
+---------+-----------------+
| NULL    |        30276687 |
| 2022-03 |           94183 |
| 2022-04 |        15696371 |
| 2022-05 |        13464425 |
| 2022-06 |        11325946 |
+---------+-----------------+


• Find average order value per customer.

=>
mysql> SELECT SUM(revenue) / COUNT(DISTINCT order_id) AS avg_order_value
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0;
+-------------------+
| avg_order_value   |
+-------------------+
| 700.0910168754693 |
+-------------------+


• Identify best-performing product categories.

=>

mysql> SELECT category, SUM(revenue) AS total_revenue
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY category
    -> ORDER BY total_revenue DESC;
+---------------+---------------+
| category      | total_revenue |
+---------------+---------------+
| Set           |      35289877 |
| kurta         |      19270568 |
| Western Dress |      10036815 |
| Top           |       4861739 |
| Ethnic Dress  |        723293 |
| Blouse        |        417333 |
| Bottom        |        136118 |
| Saree         |        120954 |
| Dupatta       |           915 |
+---------------+---------------+


• Find repeat customers and total contribution.

=>

mysql> SELECT ship_postal_code AS customer_proxy,
    ->        COUNT(DISTINCT order_id) AS order_count
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY ship_postal_code
    -> HAVING order_count > 1
    -> ORDER BY order_count DESC;

• Determine peak order days/times.

=>

mysql> SELECT DAYNAME(date) AS day_name,
    ->        COUNT(*) AS order_count
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY day_name
    -> ORDER BY order_count DESC;
+-----------+-------------+
| day_name  | order_count |
+-----------+-------------+
| NULL      |       46434 |
| Saturday  |        9329 |
| Tuesday   |        9266 |
| Wednesday |        9074 |
| Friday    |        8936 |
| Monday    |        8882 |
| Thursday  |        8451 |
| Sunday    |        8173 |
+-----------+-------------+


• Rank products by total sales and units sold.

=>

mysql> SELECT sku,
    ->        SUM(revenue) AS total_revenue,
    ->        SUM(qty) AS total_units
    -> FROM salesdata
    -> WHERE is_cancel = 0
    -> GROUP BY sku
    -> ORDER BY total_revenue DESC;
+-------------------------------+---------------+-------------+
| sku                           | total_revenue | total_units |
+-------------------------------+---------------+-------------+
| J0230-SKD-M                   |        483137 |         433 |
